### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
Genius Electronic Optical
Core resilience with CPO upside optionality
Raise PT to NT$1,250 on potential CPO contribution and intact core business
We reiterate Buy and raise our  price target to NT$1,250 from NT$700, rolling valuation
to 2028E and moving from P/B to a P/E-based SOTP that separately values the core lens
business and the emerging co-packaged optics (CPO) business. We believe Genius is
making progress sampling three precision components inside the fiber array unit (FAU)

targeting FAU integrators such as Coherent and FOCI.  We see potential for the company
to qualify the leading FAU module supplier into NVIDIA's CPO program. At the same
time, its core business is outperforming Largan, growing +1% YoY in Q326 vs. -11%
YoY for Largan as its share rises on periscope lens. Compared with the pressure on
Largan's margins from green electricity prices, we believe Genius should maintain its
margin, as its main production site is in Xiamen, whereas Largan's production in Taiwan

is affected by higher electricity prices.
Core intact: periscope share gains offset muted smartphone demand
September sales of NT$2.67bn (−2% MoM, +1% YoY) took Q326 to NT$7.82bn (+38%
QoQ, +1% YoY), in-line with VA consensus. Genius grew 10% YoY in 9M26 against 2%
at Largan, which we attribute to share gains in periscope lenses for premium iPhones,
offsetting legacy price cuts and lower VR lens shipments. We still keep 7% below VA on
4Q26E revenue (NT$7.34bn, −6% QoQ) reflecting muted iPhone sales and no content

in the iPhone 18 Pro's new variable-aperture lens.
CPO component strategy offers a different route to market from Largan
Unlike Largan's pursuit of complete FAU assembly, Genius is targeting high precision
optical components within the module, including: 1) high-precision prism to collimate
light from the PIC into the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place,
and 3) MT ferrules (high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). We

believe Genius' customer is one of the key module suppliers on NVIDIA's 2H27 program
targeting over 50% share. Our base case models a complete-FAU market of US$1.85bn
in 2028E and US$4.45bn in 2030E, with Genius base case for US$185m and US$668m
representing 10% and 15% revenue share of the complete-FAU revenue TAM.
Valuation: Maintain Buy with  PT lifted to NT$1,250
We fine-tune our 2026/2027 basic EPS estimates from NT$37.2/NT$41.5 to NT$36.7/NT

$44.0 and introduce 2028E EPS of NT$49.25. We move from P/B to P/E SOTP as the core
business now delivers steadier earnings and CPO warrants separate valuation. Our NT
$1,250 PT comprises NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) and NT$505
for CPO (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E). Catalysts include qualifications at
leading FAU makers and the board approved 7mn share private private placement with
potential strategic investor (albeit 6% dilution if issued). Our US$1.8bn CPO value

compares to US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.

### Page 2

Genius Electronic Optical UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Genius' CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
Yes, from H227E into 2028E. We see Genius' CPO strategy as feasible and lower-risk than Largan's.
Largan's goal is to supply the complete FAU; Genius is entering at the component level, supplying V-
grooves, MT ferrules and prisms to FAU makers. This lets Genius sell into multiple FAU designs and

integrators, limiting its downside if the market converges on a single winning architecture.
Management believes multi-sourcing is inevitable once volumes scale and has seen early indications
of potentially substantial demand from US optical players, including Corning and Coherent. Genius
has a partnership with FOCI and is pursuing other FAU integrators including we believe Coherent;
management suggested further updates could come in the near term. Some parts already ship in

small volumes, but we do not expect meaningful revenue until H227 or 2028.
Q: Will Genius benefit from further lens technology upgrades in the coming years?
Yes. We expect premium lens participation and product mix to support core revenue growth of 8.0%,
9.7% and 9.1% in 2026-28E. We believe Genius is gaining share from Largan in Apple's periscope
lens supply chain after initially lagging. Apple (70–80% of Genius' sales) sell-through units grew 9%

YoY YTD through August according to Counterpoint, and Genius' 9M26 revenue grew 10% YoY on
a similar trajectory. Largan grew only 2% in 9M26, with Q326 down 11% YoY. For the iPhone 18
series, Genius participates in every lens spec except the variable-aperture upgrade, where it stepped
back because of pricing pressure from Chinese competitors. Management says Genius has the
technical capability and could enter if future specs or design changes improve the opportunity.

Genius offers a combination of an established earnings base from smartphone lens and potential
exposure to the passive components required for optical interconnects. We remain constructive on
the lens business and see CPO as a potentially material addition from 2028. The component strategy
provides several routes to customers, but the valuation should reflect uncertainty over qualified
content, allocation and margins. Our Buy rating and NT$1,250 PT is based  on NT$745 base business

(15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505 for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in
2028E). The CPO option for Genius is US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.
Genius share gains are showing in reported sales, with YTD sales +10% YoY vs. +2% YoY for Largan.
The company is also participating in every spec except variable aperture.  For CPO, Genius delivered
samples of its prism, V-groove, and MT ferrule to FOCI at the end of May, with initial feedback broadly

on track and no major issues reported to date. Our checks indicate the company is also positioning to
supply one of the leading FAU module suppliers for NVIDIA's CPO program from H227.
At NT$1,060, the shares trade at 24.1x our 2027E core EPS and 21.5x our 2028E core EPS, factoring
some of the CPO opportunity in the multiple rather than in estimates relative to the 10 year average
17x P/E. Applying a 15x valuation to the smartphone lens business + 40x to the CPO option from our

scenario of NT$9.30 EPS in 2028E yields upside from the last close at NT$1,060 to a NT$1,250 share
price.

With CPO revenue contribution <5% in 2026 per management guidance, Genius' lens
business should remain its primary earnings anchor. With foldables shipping in 2H, Q3

shipment orders are broadly in line with historical patterns but should be watchful on
near-term demand trends as customers tend to adjust orders quickly in response to new
product sell-through evidenced by Largan's recent cuts. VR contribution has declined
from 5–10% in 2025 to <5% in 2026 on demand slowdown, partially offsetting
strength in phone lens while the mix shift would enhance Genius' overall margin profile.
On the other hand, AR is seeing traction supported by ongoing Ray-Ban shipments with

next-gen products in development.

### Page 4

Genius position in the CPO value chain
Genius is targeting the CPO market as a component supplier while Largan is advancing
from components into full-FAU assembly. Genius is sampling three components for CPO
with FAU makers, including: 1) high-precision prism to redirect light from the PIC into
the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place, and 3) MT ferrules
(high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). The companies overlap in

capability, with Largan also having PMLA and fiber arrays but not getting into some
components like MT ferrules and V-Grooves.
Genius's optical design, precision tooling, metrology and volume-manufacturing
experience offer it a good entry point for this market. Commercial differentiation will
depend on reproducible optical performance, dimensional tolerances, thermal stability
and customer yield.  It has announced collaboration with FOCI and  we believe has

potential to also work with one of NVIDIA's leading module suppliers for its H227
program.

Annual FAU TAM reaches US$1.85bn in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E in

our base case
We update our FAU (fiber array unit) TAM factoring CPO/NPO penetration into
AI networking switches, NVIDIA multi-rack systems, scale-up on compute and switch
boards, and non-NVIDIA accelerators.  We assume one passive FAU assembly connecting
a fiber array to a photonic engine with one unit per optical engine.
We assume one FAU per optical engine and 32 per switch ASIC in our base case,
matching NVIDIA's 32x3.2T engines on its 102.4T Spectrum-X package. Broadcom's

102.4Tb/s Davisson uses 16 engines at 6.4Tb/s, so our bear case assumes an even mix of
these two designs (24 per ASIC). We note that higher switch bandwidth does not
automatically increase FAU count if engine bandwidth rises at the same rate. Our 2030E
assumption maintains the 32 FAUs per switch based on bandwidth rising to 204.8Tb/s

Our base case for the FAU market sums up 4 opportunities for CPO/NPO attach:
1. Scale-out and scale-across switches
We estimate annual switch ASIC shipments x CPO + NPO penetration x optical engines
per ASIC x FAUs per engine. In 2028E, 25% CPO/NPO penetration or 1.0m 102.4T
switch ASICs at 32 FAUs each produces 8.0m FAUs. In 2030E, 55% of 1.1mn

204.8T switch ASICs at 32 FAUs drives a market size of 19.4mn FAUs.

2. NVIDIA's multi-rack connections for Rubin Ultra and Feynman
NVIDIA at its 2026 GTC San Jose proposed a multi-rack option to network together 8
Oberon NVL72 racks for the Rubin Ultra generation and 8 Kyber NVL144 racks.

Feedback at the time showed  possible transceiver or NPO connections for Rubin Ultra
and CPO connections for Feynman.  Our 2028 base case uses 110,000 racks, 15% multi-
rack adoption and 75% CPO/NPO penetration, giving approximately 12,400 connected
racks.
For 2030E, our rack count falls to 35,000 racks as we assume Kyber would have 4x the
number of GPUs, equivalent to 140k Oberon equivalents. We assume the 35,000 racks
at 55% multi-rack adoption x 80% CPO/NPO attach gives 15,400 connected racks. We

assume about 216 and 576 inter-rack FAUs per rack, respectively, driving a market
opportunity of 2.7mn FAUs in 2028E and 8.9mn in 2030E.

4. Non NVIDIA scale-up opportunity

Overall market opportunity: 12.3mn/35.6mn generates a US$1.85bn/US
$4.5bn TAM in 2028/2030E
We sum four pools: scale-out/scale-across switches, NVIDIA multi-rack pods, CPO native
NVIDIA GPUs and non-NVIDIA scale-up.  We add 10%/5% for FAUs lost at downstream
attach and test and for field spares, driving a market size of in our base case of 12.3mn
FAUs in 2028E and 35.6mn in 2030E. At US$150/US$125 per FAU, that is a US$1.85bn

market in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E (bear/bull US$0.5bn/US$3.5bn in 2028E; US
$1.4bn/US$9.0bn in 2030E). The expansion to 2030E comes from wider optical
adoption and greater content in scale-up systems, despite an assumed FAU ASP decline
from US$150 to US$125.

Scenario analysis on Genius CPO opportunity

### Page 7

We assume in base case Genius captures 10% of the TAM in 2028E and 15% by 2030E.
The base case in 2028E is consistent with US$30 of prism, V-groove and ferrule content
in half of all FAUs or US$15 average content spread across the FAU market.  The
2030E bull case (25%) or US$37.50 would require near full content of some of the sub-
components or otherwise ability to integrate more of the solution value by that time.
Our bear case assumes only 1% share of the opportunity in 2028E and 5% of the

opportunity in 2030E, with EPS even by 2030E falling to NT$4 and yielding limited
valuation potential for the shares.

Reiterate Buy and raise PT to NT$1,250 valuing in CPO optionality

We adjust our valuation method from P/B to P/E SOTP to better reflect the company’s
core business and its opportunity within CPO industry while the core smartphone
business is demonstrating more stable earnings to use P/E vs. prior P/B. Our PT at NT
$1,250 is based on NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505
for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E) with catalysts from
upcoming qualifications into leading FAU makers and potential strategic private

placement with board approving 7mn share private placement (albeit 6% dilution if
issued). We note Genius CPO option valued at US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our
Largan valuation.
Our price target uses 2028 earnings for both businesses, reflecting the modeled start of
meaningful CPO contribution. We apply 15x core EPS of NT$49.25 and 40x CPO EPS of
NT$12.72 for a NT$1,250 price target. The 40x multiple assumes the optical business
can sustain strong growth after its initial ramp; qualification, concentration and margin

risk limit the confidence that can be placed on that earnings stream.
In the upside case, stronger premium lens participation and manufacturing execution lift
2028E core EPS to NT$54.32. Faster optical adoption expands the 2028E FAU market to
US$3.46bn; 15% effective revenue capture and a 31% pretax margin produce
approximately NT$36.88 CPO EPS. Applying 15x to core earnings and 42x to CPO
earnings gives approximately NT$2,365.
In the downside case, weaker lens demand and margin pressure lower core EPS to NT

$44.29. Slower optical adoption leaves a US$0.46bn FAU market, of which Genius
captures 1%, producing only NT$0.29 CPO EPS. Applying 12x and 38x gives
approximately NT$540. The downside is driven by both a weaker core business and a
much smaller optical contribution.

### Page 10

Genius Electronic Optical UBS Research
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Genius (3406.TW) trading at 1,060 at closed Oct 8.
Source: UBS estimates
UPSIDE (NT$2,365): Lens upgrades accelerate and Genius gains share in higher-end
specs. 2027E/28E sales grow 11.8%/9.1% YoY (base 9.7%/9.1%), and GM expands to
35.6%/35.5% (base 34.4%/34.3%) on better yields and mix. 2028E core EPS of NT
$54.3 (+10% vs base) at 15x gives NT$815. Faster CPO adoption and stronger AI capex

lift the 2028E FAU TAM to US$3.5bn (19.8mn units at US$175); at 15% capture, CPO
sales reach US$519mn and CPO EPS NT$36.9, worth NT$1,550 at 42x.
BASE (NT$1,250):  Core NT$745 (15x NT$49.25) plus CPO NT$505 (40x NT$12.72), as
above.
DOWNSIDE (NT540): Smartphone demand weakens, legacy price erosion continues
and share gains stall. 2027E sales grow 7.4% YoY with GM of 33.4%, and 2028E core
EPS falls to NT$44.3 (−10% vs base), worth NT$530 at 12x. CPO adoption is slow

(10%/3% CPO/NPO in scale-out, no ASIC attach), leaving a US$0.46bn TAM; at 1%
capture, CPO is worth NT$11.

Company Description
Founded in 1989, Genius Electronic Optical (GSEO) is a leading global manufacturer of
camera lenses, providing full service optical solutions, including mobile lenses, automotive
lenses and AR/VR optics. Its production sites are in Taichung (one plant) and Xiamen (two

plants). The Taichung plant mainly manufactures VR lenses, while the Xiamen plants produce
compact lenses for consumer electronics. Optical lens products accounted for 73% of 2023
total revenue, and others include TOF lenses, flash lenses, VR lenses and LED lighting
products. Its major client is Apple and it supplies lenses for iPhones and iPads.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's

interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our Genius price target is based on PE-based sum-of-the-parts model. Genius' growth is
highly leveraged to the overall handset industry and could be affected by volatility in handset
demand, as well as whether handset OEMs will continue upgrading their camera design.

Genius' margins could be affected by increasing competition and the rise of new
technologies, ASP pressure from customers and fluctuating material and labour costs.
Downside risks include competitors catching up in technology, yield rate issues for new
products and uncertain iPhone demand.
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business. We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from

smartphone OEM clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a
slower-thanexpected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success
below the market's elevated expectations. We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-
expected CPO qualification process at Largan. In addition, if the company demonstrates
exceptionally efficient and highly accurate automation capabilities, it could support greater
market share gains within the industry than we currently anticipate.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the

嘉澤：全球前三大 CPU Socket 供應商，伺服器已成營收主軸
全球前三大中央處理器插座CPU socket供應商。依Bishop統計在電腦暨週邊連
接器廠商中全球排名第四，不分行業別的全球連接器排名全球排名第十八。
主要客戶為AMD與Intel。產品涵蓋 CPU Socket、記憶體插槽、高速連接器及
線材，並延伸至 SOCAMM、液冷快接頭與 NPO Socket。
本研究預估，嘉澤伺服器營收由 2026 年221.14 億元增至 2027 年 328.67 億元，
年增 48.6%。成長來自板量回升、CPU新平台提高 Socket 與高速連接器價值，
以及 AI 新產品放量；PC 相關營收占比則由 2025 年 36.60%，降至 2027 年
16.5%，營運重心持續轉向伺服器。

有助於防止潛在的損壞。嘉澤主要客戶為AMD與Intel。

代理 AI 讓 CPU 工作量增加：從回覆問題，走向執行任務
原先：用戶提出問題；應用程式向模型發送提示；模型生成答案；應用程式將答
案傳回給用戶。主節點CPU負責調度、I/O和系統管理，而GPU則負責繁重的運
算任務。
現在：代理AI不再只是回答一個問題，而是將目標分解成多個步驟，決定下一步
該做什麼，調用多個模型，查詢資料庫，連接API，運行企業應用程序，檢查權
限，檢索內存，驗證輸出，然後再次循環執行整個過程。
CPU負責：編排：管理分解複雜任務的引擎。代理執行和工具呼叫：觸發 API 和
傳統企業軟體。策略與安全：對每個自主操作進行實際檢查。
隨 AI 從模型推論延伸至 Agent 執行，CPU 的角色擴及任務協調、工具操作及資
料處理。AMD 5 月官方文章已描述 CPU：GPU 由過去約 1：4–8，朝 1：1 移
動；Intel Q2 法說進一步表示，目前以顆數計已接近均衡。兩家原廠的觀察，支
持 CPU 在 AI 基礎建設中重要性提高

資料來源 :AMD官網(智能體人工智慧改變了CPU/GPU的平衡)
5

作」的分散式架構。未來不是只增加 GPU，而是新增獨立的 Agentic CPU
compute layer
New Agentic CPU Racks：新增的 Agent 執行層負責 Agent Control
Plane 與 Tool Execution管理 agent 的任務拆解、工作流調度、狀態管理與
多 agent 協作執行 API 呼叫、SQL 查詢、文件檢索、資料處理、程式執行與

能是把 LLM 的「決策」轉換成企業系統中的「實際行動」

Meta Muse：個人代理產品化，使 CPU 執行環境的需求更具體
Muse 的投資意義在於：大型消費平台開始提供可持續執行任務的個人代理，使
CPU 沙盒需求從技術架構走向實際服務。
與AMD的關係Meta已部署數百萬顆AMD EPYC，並是Venice、Verano 的領先
客戶。因此，Meta 的運算擴張是 AMD CPU 需求的重要觀察對象。
持續在線的虛擬機（Persistent VMs）： Meta Muse 的底層採用了 Muse
Secure VM 架構。這些 VM 即使在使用者沒有主動點開 App 時，也在後台持續
運作，消耗大量的作業系統配置、記憶體管理與安全驗證資源—這些全部屬於通
用運算，只有 CPU 能做。因此當億萬名用戶的 AI 代理同時在線，CPU 的短缺
會遠比 GPU 來得更強烈。Muse 這樣的個人代理規模不斷擴大，對 CPU 的需求
勢必會增長，預計 CPU 與 GPU 的比例將從 1:1往4:1以上走 (intel電話會議)

資料來源 :trendforce, meta
對運算需求的意義Muse 是甚麼
大型平台投入代理服務產品
化

AI 代理
執行環境需要 CPU、記憶體
及系統資源
使用專屬安全虛擬機 Muse

工具操作與程式執行增加具備瀏覽器，可跨應用處理工作

Meta muse帶動AI agent趨勢，使CPU類股上漲
 Meta Muse 在 9 月 8 日剛上線時，市場仍在觀望它與傳統的問答機器人（如 ChatGPT）
有何不同。直到 9 月 18 日（上線第 10 天），第三方研調機構 Sensor Tower 釋出數據，
顯示 Muse 已經連續數天登頂美國 App Store 免費榜冠軍，13 天內累計下載突破 250 萬
次。這時市場才驚覺，這是一個「現象級爆款」。
 Muse 可以實際幫用戶串接 Gmail、自動比價，甚至與 Shopify 的 Shop Pay 整合代客結
帳。當週一（9 月 21 日）開盤，市場資金首先推升 Meta 股價大漲 11%。CPU類股全面
大漲10%，嘉澤隔日也開盤即漲停，顯示市場有定位上的連動。(如下圖)
 截至9/30，目前該應用僅在美國與加拿大上線2 2天突破了 500 萬次下載。相比之下，
ChatGPT 達到 500 萬次下載花了 56 天，Grok 花了 103 天，Claude 則耗時 492 天。市

場發現 AI 正從「回答問題」走向「替人類實際操作」的實際應用，引發了 AI Agent的邁
向普及的轉折點。

 Venice 高階 SP7 平台由運算核心、記憶體通道到高速傳輸全面升級。對嘉澤而言，成

新平台滲透率提高將帶動產品組合改善。新平台配置的記憶體模組由 24 條增加至 32 條，
多出 8 個使用中的記憶體插槽位置。這說明平台升級除了提高 CPU Socket 規格，也可
能增加每台伺服器的記憶體連接器用量。

開發NPO socket，為未來潛在商機
資料來源 :moneyDJ、利成光電科技有限公司
圖片來源：OIF 的《Co-Packaging Framework》第 10 頁、圖 2(d) 21
NPO的核心定義是：將光引擎從傳統的前面板可插拔位置，移動到更靠近交
換晶片（ASIC） 的地方，但光引擎和ASIC仍然是兩個獨立的封裝體。它們通
常被並排安裝在同一塊高性能基板（如矽中介層或有機基板）上
NPO Socket已進入客戶最後認證階段，且達可量產狀態，量產目標落在2027
年第3季，目前未納入估計，等待未來公司給更多指引。
AI資料中心在Scale-up與Scale-out兩種架構同步拉高頻寬需求，嘉澤在NPO、
既有高速銅傳輸與高功率連接方案的產品組合，將對應不同平台導入時程。

研 究  報 告                
貿聯-KY(3665.TT) 
產業：電子–其他電子 
正式合併 Interplex 財報，大幅挹注 HPC 產品營收 
 投資評價與建議 
正式併購Interplex，預估2026/2027年稅後EPS為70.58/128.86元，過往貿聯
本益比位於17~30倍區間，考量併購與光通訊專案逐步發酵，給予25倍PER。 
 重點評論分析 
正式併購Interplex集團Datacom業務，大幅挹注HPC營收：近期貿聯正式合
併Interplex集團資料通訊業務，並自2026年9月23日起納入合併報表。  
Interplex產品包含：伺服器機殼、機櫃、滑軌與CPU Socket等，併購有利提
升貿聯在單⼀機櫃之產值，另外有利切入過往較難打進供應鏈之CSP客戶。 
HPC和半導體設備仍為主要獲利引擎 ， 光通訊佈局逐步發酵 ：展望2026~2027
年 ， 營收動能主要來自 ： (1)Ethernet AEC規格逐漸從400G往800G迭代 ， 800G 
AEC主要採用客戶為xAI與AWS，1.6T AEC預計將於4Q26隨Nvidia Rubin新
平台開始正式出貨，ALC主動式光纜目前已有2個以上客戶表示願意採用，預
估2H27開始貢獻營收 ； (2)Vera Rubin機櫃功率突破300kW ， 帶動Power Whip
規格從60A提升至100A 、 機櫃內Busbar規格從1,400A提升至5,000Ａ ， 電源規
格提升帶動產品單價隨安培數增加而提升；(3)Rubin Ultra機櫃Power Rack設
計帶動Power Whip用量與ASP皆顯著增加 ， 預估產值成⻑2倍以上 ； (4)貿聯與
ams OSRAM合作開發microVCSEL互連方案，另外與SENKO和ficonTEC合作
全自動化光學互連與光纖製備流程，目前光通訊營收已佔整體營收約2~3%。 
 投資風險 
伺服器新平台出貨進度不如預期、原物料成本上漲。

2

| 營運概況  
2Q26毛利率重回成⻑軌道，HPC與半導體設備需求持續強勁，3Q26營收創高：2Q26營收季增11.6%至232.6億元，係
因HPC與半導體設備業務需求持續強勁，1Q26毛利率僅28.8%，主要受車用等非AI低毛利產品復甦導致產品組合改變
以及xAI因併購使其資料中心建置進度停滯 ， 導致延後拉貨AEC與power產品 ， 貿聯產能利用率下降亦影響毛利率 ， 2Q26
毛利率季增1.96ppts至30.7%，重回過往水準，係因客戶逐步恢復拉貨，稅後EPS為15.28元。貿聯8月營收月減7.7%至
88.9億元，係因歐洲地區歷經季節性暑期⻑假，9月營收月增5%至93.3億元，營收達成率101%，符合預期，係因xAI逐
步恢復拉貨以及AWS Trainium 3放量，3Q26營收季增19.8%至278.9億元，預估稅後EPS為19.12元。 
正式併購Interplex集團Datacom業務，大幅挹注HPC營收： 貿聯-KY於2026年6月宣布斥資8.5億美元收購新加坡
Interplex集團資料通訊業務，近期正式合併並自2026年9月23日起納入合併報表。Interplex營運總部位於新加坡，產能
遍及南亞與亞洲地區，包括：泰國、越南、⾺來⻄亞與中國等，其核心技術為精密金屬加工與機構件，主要產品包含：
伺服器機殼、機櫃、滑軌與CPU Socket等，另外僅Busbar產品線與貿聯重疊且同為Nvidia MGX合格供應商，併購除擴
張HPC營收規模外，亦提升貿聯在單⼀AI Server機櫃之產值，另外透過併購有利切入過往較難打進供應鏈之CSP客戶。
2025年Interplex營收約4億美元，佔貿聯2025年營收之17%，隨AI運算與推論需求增加，假設Interplex 2026和2027年
營收皆年增30%，預估可貢獻貿聯2026/2027年營收分別約39與200億元。 
HPC 和半導體設備仍為主要獲利引擎，光通訊佈局逐步發酵，成為「銅光並進」主要受惠者：展望 2026~2027 年，貿
聯營收動能主要來自：(1)隨 AI 頻寬需求增加，Ethernet AEC 規格逐漸從 400G 往 800G 迭代，預計 2026 年底前成為
主流規格，800G AEC 主要採用客戶為 xAI 與 AWS，其中 xAI 因 AEC 採用規格最高且⻑度最⻑，故 ASP 較高，1.6T 
AEC 預計將於 4Q26 隨 Nvidia Rubin 新平台開始正式出貨，由於 1.6T AEC 採用 8 條單通道 200G 之線材組裝，故組
裝難度提升且成本翻倍，因此 ASP 和毛利率皆優於過往規格，另外 PCIe AEC 則已開發完成，將隨 CSP 推出 PCIe 版本
Switch 放量，ALC 主動式光纜目前已有 2 個以上客戶表示願意採用，預估 2H27 開始貢獻營收；(2)隨 Vera Rubin 機櫃
功率突破 300kW，帶動 Power Whip 規格從 60A 提升至 100A、機櫃內 Busbar 規格從 1,400A 提升至 5,000Ａ，電源
規格提升帶動產品單價隨安培數增加而提升；(3)Rubin Ultra 機櫃可容納 576 顆 GPU 使單⼀機櫃功耗超過 500kW．由
於機櫃無法容納電源設備，故單獨拉出成為 Power Rack．帶動「Power Rack 至運算機櫃」電源連接需求大幅增加，預
估 Power Whip 之用量與 ASP 皆顯著增加 ， 預估產值成⻑超過 2 倍以上 ； (4)光通訊領域方面 ， 近期貿聯與 ams OSRAM
合作開發 AI scale-up microVCSEL 互連方案，另外持續與 SENKO 和 ficonTEC 合作全自動化的光學互連與光纖製備流
程，目前光通訊營收已佔公司整體營收約 2~3%，顯示光通訊產品成⻑⼒道強勁。綜上所述，預估 2026/2027 年營收
年增 49.5%/55.4%至 1,064.9/1,654.6 億元，稅後 EPS 為 70.58/128.86 元。

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

百和(9938) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：最壞已過且股價低於淨值，靜待基本面起飛

一、3Q26 自結 EPS 0.62 元，低於預期；主要受到網
布毛利率下滑且業外利息費用所致 
百和 3Q26 營收 44.09 億元(+15.9% QoQ / +5.8% YOY)，
就各產品比重來看，黏扣帶 12.3%、織鞋帶 44.1%、鬆
緊帶 13%、射出勾 6.6%、針織經編提花網布 20.1%以
及其他 3.9%。3Q26 就各產品線來看，所有產品線均成
長，僅有經編網布衰退，其中鬆緊帶+16%YoY、黏扣帶
+9.7%YoY、織鞋帶+9% YOY、射出勾+6.7%YoY 以及
針織經編提花網布-8.4% YOY。獲利方面，3Q26 毛利
率 29.5%，與 2Q26 約略持平，也較3Q25 下滑 6.3%，
主要反映關稅影響、產品組合以及受到美伊戰爭影響原
物料成本上揚所致；就 3Q26 各產品線毛利率來看，粘
扣帶 27.6%、 鬆緊帶37.9%、 射出勾44%、 織鞋帶30.4%、
經編網布 17.2％以及其他 37.5%。其中經編網布毛利率
較 2Q26 回升 2.7%，但仍低於 2025 年水準，主要反映
產品組合差異以及稼動率的影響；3Q26 合併營業利益
達 4.35 億元，營業利益率 9.9%，業外淨損 1.41 億元，
主要反映百和-KY 利息費用，稅前淨利 2.94 億元，稅後
歸屬母公司淨利 1.84 億元，EPS 0.62 元。  
二 、 考量原物料成本提升且通膨持續影響全球消費景氣 ，
下修百和 2026 年營收為低個位數增長且因毛利率表現
低於預期，下修 2026 年 EPS 為 2.48 元 
9M26 前十大客戶佔比 65.5%，德國品牌仍為第一大客
戶 ， 佔比25.1%，YOY-5%、 美系品牌15.7%，YOY+2%，
其中鞋子持平、服飾類+11%、瑞士品牌為第三大客戶，
佔營收比重 6.1%，YOY-11%，New Balance 占比 4.4%，
YOY-11%。HOKA 已為前十大客戶 ， 佔營收比重2.3%，
YOY+58%，為前九月成長性最佳客戶。就產品應用別
來看 ，前九月Footwear 占比 60.4%、Apparel 25.3%、
3C 2.8%； 以前九個月YOY 來看 ，Footwear-1% YOY、
Apparel +12% YOY、3C 產品+16% YOY 以及醫療-3% 
YOY，但單就第三季表現來看，Footwear 持平、
Apparel+27% YOY、3C+20% YOY 以及醫療+31% 
YOY，顯示其他應用端需求起來，未來百和將持續著重
其他類別發展，不單僅限於鞋子跟衣服，醫療跟 3C 已
漸有成效。過去 3C 是客人指定材料，百和僅有加工；
但現在幾乎 80%都是百和的材料進行加工，做成客戶想
要的東西，附加價值較高。3C 及醫療目前營收占比約
6%，未來可望緩步增加。 
考量美伊戰爭引發原物料成本提升且戰爭仍未平息影響
全球消費景氣，輔以客戶下單仍顯保守；我們預估百和
2026 年營收為 161.25 億元 (+2.2% YoY)，其中傳產中
個位數成長、網布中~高個位數衰退；毛利率方面，考量
原物料價格上揚因素未解、關稅影響持續且經編網布毛
利率低於預期 ， 保守預估2026 年集團毛利率為 30.7%，
較 2025 年下滑 4.6%，下修本業獲利為 17.68 億元，營
業利益率 11%，業外利息費用與 2025 年大致相當，預
估稅後歸屬母公司淨利潤 7.41 億元 ， 下修EPS 為 2.48
元。 
資本支出方面，2025 年實際資本支出 19.01 億元，其中
廠房建物 26％、設備購置 72%以及其他 2%。9M26 資
本支出 22.69 億元，其中台灣百和3.31 億元、百和興業
19.38 億元；2026 年預期資本支出約 28.02 億元，主要
以百和興業為主，約占 3/4；台灣百和約 1/4，主要用在
機器設備。 
三、下修經編緹花網布 2026 年至中~高個位數衰退，長
期仍可望有轉單效益 
2025 年隨著品牌採用意願提升，網布營收貢獻達 34.08
億元，YOY+38%，毛利率 34.09%；2026 年原預估在
新產能以及核心客戶、新興客戶放量且打入成衣供應鏈
帶動，成長性不亞於 2025 年，但受到全球大環境不確
定性高且受到產品組合以及主要客戶新一代跑鞋主料轉
用梭織且非由百和-KY 供應影響，前九個月網布營收衰
4.9%，我們下修 2026 年經編緹花網布營收為 31.46 億
元，-7.68% YOY， 毛利率受到稼動率偏低且產品組合影
響，前三季僅 19.9%。現在網布往圓編、橫編等更多織
法開發，未來可望持續擴大應用面，未來隨著應用面擴
大且稼動率提升，毛利率有機會往 2025 年水準邁進。 
網布客戶占比來看 ，2025 年德系品牌仍是第一大客戶，

四、維持買進理由 
2026 年受到美伊戰爭引發原物料成本提升且戰爭仍未
平息影響全球消費景氣，輔以客戶下單仍顯保守；我們
預估百和 2026 年營收為 161.25 億元 (+2.2% YoY)，其
中傳產中個位數成長 、 網布中~高個位數衰退 ， 下修EPS
為 2.48 元。展望 2027 年，隨著品牌對應庫存調整到較
健康的水位，下單可望回到正常模式。大環境穩定後，
原物料成本可以趨於正常、關稅議題減緩，2027 年可望
優於 2026 年，預估 2027 年 EPS 3.23 元。股價方面，
考量最壞已過 、2027 年可望恢復成長且目前股價低於淨
值，故維持買進評等，目標價 48 元(15X 2027PER)。

## Document 216: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속원-_fba73583

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속원-_fba73583.pdf

2026.09.21   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다.

 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

## Document 257: CEE & CCA week ahead_ Hungarian and Czech growth, plus CCA rate decisions

Source PDF: all report pdf/CEE & CCA week ahead_ Hungarian and Czech growth, plus CCA rate decisions.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Hungary and the Czech Republic will release third-quarter GDP
estimates next week, while Uzbekistan and Azerbaijan
announce their latest monetary policy decisions

Peter Virovacz, David Havrlant and Dmitry Dolgin
Hungary's growth picture remains mixed
Hungary: Flash GDP to provide ﬁrst look at
third-quarter growth
Friday brings the release of the preliminary Q3 GDP estimate. We
expect agriculture to have weighed heavily on growth in the third
quarter, but industry – mostly driven by the new export capacities –
should largely oﬀset this. Services will remain a major positive
contributor.
As this is only a ﬂash estimate, the data will provide few details.
Even so, annual growth should remain robust despite the quarterly
slowdown. We therefore maintain our full-year growth forecast of
1.9% and continue to expect growth to pick up in the ﬁnal quarter
of the year.

### Page 2

Czech Republic: Conﬁdence likely to
weaken as growth softens
Monday brings the release of October conﬁdence indicators, and on
Friday we'll get the preliminary 3Q26 GDP estimate. We expect
economic performance to have weakened further on an annual
basis in 3Q26, but quarter-on-quarter growth should have picked
up thanks to a pre-stocking eﬀect triggered by the Hormuz conﬂict.
Large numbers of people submitted new orders when it became
clear that prices would rise and products would become more

scarce.
That said, we assume this positive eﬀect will fade at the year's turn.
Both business and consumer conﬁdence likely deteriorated in
October, reﬂecting rising prices, the bond-market selloﬀ, the
propensity for tighter monetary policy, and the crisis in France.
CCA: Uzbekistan and Azerbaijan central
banks expected to stay on hold
Wednesday brings the Central Bank of Uzbekistan's latest interest
rate decision. We expect the CBRU to keep the policy rate

unchanged at 14.00%, although a 50bp cut remains a close
alternative. A hold appears appropriate given an increasingly pro-
inﬂationary and hawkish global backdrop, some recent weakening
in the soum and still-strong domestic demand. At the same time,
the case for easing continues to build, as core inﬂation has recently
stabilised at 5.5% YoY, not far above the 5% target, real interest
rates remain high, and domestic bank funding continues to
materially outperform lending growth, pointing to restrictive

monetary conditions. We therefore see a hold as a tactical pause
before gradual easing in the medium term.
The Central Bank of Azerbaijan announces its latest interest rate
decision on Thursday. We expect the CBA to keep the reﬁnancing
rate unchanged at 6.50%, as inﬂation remains within the 4±2%
target range. Headline CPI eased to 5.5% YoY in September, arguing
against tightening. However, CPI remains relatively close to the
upper end of the target range, while domestic producer-price

## Document 259: Cliff Notes_ getting the balance right (2026-10-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ getting the balance right (2026-10-23).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: getting the balance right
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
10:46 October 16 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
Australia’s key data release this week was the September Labour Force Survey. Employment surprised
to the downside, -39.3k. However, a 0.2ppt decline in labour force participation from August’s near-
historic high largely offset. The unemployment rate therefore held at 4.7% after August was revised up.

Residual seasonality may be in the mix. Regardless, the take home point from the update is that the

### Page 2

labour market is continuing to ease as employment growth runs below working-age population growth.
We expect the unemployment rate to grind higher into 2027 as tighter financial conditions and cost-of-
living pressures restrict activity growth to a below-trend pace. Although, our forecast peak is near at
4.8%, due by Q2 2027.
This update on the labour market complicates the communication challenge for the RBA. The
September minutes were firmly on the hawkish side, with the discussion focussing on materialising

upside risks to inflation. But, with the unemployment rate now through the RBA’s NAIRU assumption of
4.6%, Governor Bullock’s “a little tight” descriptor for the labour market is becoming harder to defend.
Still, with a clear focus on inflation risks – which also led us to revise up our near-term CPI forecasts in
the October Market Outlook – we still expect a 25bp rate hike at the November meeting.
Over in the US, the headline CPI rose 0.6% as expected in September, taking the annual rate up to

3.7%yr. Energy prices were a key contributor (5.2%, 20.4%yr), while core inflation was a touch above
consensus in the month at 0.3% (2.6%yr). Within the core components, core goods prices gained 0.3%
after being broadly unchanged over the past 6 months (0.7%yr). Core services meanwhile rose 0.4%
(3.1%yr) on a 0.4% increase in shelter (3.2%yr), a 0.6% gain for transport services and a 0.3%
increase in medical care services (2.7%yr and 2.5%yr respectively). Further, the subsequent release of

the PPI pointed to continued upstream price pressures, with the headline and core measures up
6.3%yr and 5.0%yr respectively. The latest Beige Book provided colour on these developments, firms
across multiple districts citing tariffs, insurance and commodity prices as ongoing sources of cost
pressure. Guiding on the risks, many respondents expect further price increases as contracts/hedges
roll off.
Given their telegraphed concerns over inflation, there is enough signal in these reports to warrant a

follow-up hike at the October FOMC meeting. Though it seems Committee members believe they are
near the end of the tightening cycle and so have time on their side. This week, Daly and Bowman both
showed patience in assessing the trend, with no clear bias towards an October or December decision.
Williams stated he believes one more hike would prove sufficient in this cycle given known information.
And Waller also showed confidence, highlighting that elevated long-term yields are doing some of the

FOMC’s work, restricting demand in interest rate sensitive sectors. We hold a similar view, anticipating
that one more hike by year end and a little time will bring inflation near the 2.0%yr medium-term target.
However, as discussed in this month’s Market Outlook, capacity pressures are likely to see inflation
consistently above target, rather than below, in coming years – particularly if, as we suspect, capacity
in the housing sector remains tight. This will mean the FOMC have to remain modestly restrictive over

the forecast horizon and will also bias up long-term yields.
In Asia, there was one release that stood out, China’s trade account. At USD133.5bn, the September
result was a whisker inside January 2025’s record high of USD137.9bn. More impressively, throughout
2025 and 2026 year-to-date the surplus has now averaged USD100.9bn, almost 3 times the average of
2019, prior to the pandemic. The geographic breadth of export growth is as impressive as the headline

annual gain of 36.3%yr, even more so given the ongoing strength in Chinese investment in
neighbouring nations’ production chains. In time, both trade and income receipts from offshore
investment should buoy the renminbi, although for now continued outbound foreign investment should
moderate the pace of gains against other nations in the region, and likely versus the US dollar.

### Page 3

Related articles
Another key trend assessed in October’s Market Outlook is the evolution of producer prices across
Asia, and the likelihood of the disinflationary benefit the west has received in recent decades
continuing. Historically, labour cost arbitrage was one of the key drivers of declining goods prices.
While still possible, we expect the next phase of manufacturing development to instead be driven by
capital, technology and energy. Many end users and the manufacturers undertaking production are

also increasingly prioritising resilience, security and specialisation over cost minimisation. It is therefore
more probably future supply-chain developments will help contain rising costs through productivity than
drive sustained price declines for the goods bought by consumers and businesses.

## Document 278: ECB caught in the French drama

Source PDF: all report pdf/ECB caught in the French drama.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The European Central Bank meeting on 29 October is not
expected to result in an interest rate hike. Instead, the French
crisis is increasingly eroding the need for additional rate hikes
Carsten Brzeski
With the surge in bond yields on the back of the crisis in France, we expect the
ECB to remain on hold at next week's meeting
Back in September, the ECB surprised markets with a more hawkish
message, at least between the lines. With headline inﬂation higher

for longer and core inﬂation expected to increase in 2027, the stage
seemed set for at least one more rate hike at the December
meeting. Some even speculated about a back-to-back increase in
October.
One month later, and after a severe ﬁscal and political crisis in
France, the ECB is unlikely to repeat the hawkish tone at next week’s
meeting. At face value, macro developments since the last meeting
would still justify another rate hike. Headline inﬂation increased

again in September and most leading indicators point to continued
resilience in the eurozone economy. However, the surge in bond

### Page 2

yields on the back of the French crisis is already imposing a new
restrictive monetary policy stance. In fact, the surge in German
bond yields since the September meeting has already had roughly
the same impact on growth and inﬂation as an additional 25bp
rate hike. For France, it's closer to a 50bp hike. Or in other words, the
bond market has taken over the ECB’s job.
All of this means that the ECB is very likely to stay on hold next
week and will delay any next rate hike decision to December. This is

when new staﬀ projections will be available and when there will
also be more insights into whether higher energy prices will
eventually bring knock-on eﬀects on the rest of the economy or
not. In fact, there are three main parameters that, in our view, will
determine the ECB’s path going forward: i) the length and intensity
of the war in the Middle East. The longer oil prices remain elevated,
the higher the probability of more rate hikes; ii) the knock-on eﬀects

of higher energy prices. So far, there has hardly been any pass-
through of higher energy prices in the rest of the economy. If this
remains the case, the need for further rate hikes drops. If it doesn’t,
the likelihood of new rate hikes goes up. iii) France. Any solution to
the current French ﬁscal woes could bring some relief to bond
markets and allow the ECB to continue hiking rates. Without any
solution, bond markets have taken over the ECB’s role, making rate

hikes less likely (and less needed).
Because it's France
Even if France does not play a role in the ECB’s policy statement
next week, it will be an important element at the press conference.
Political and ﬁscal concerns in France, and the resulting widening in
French spreads, have raised questions about whether the ECB could
intervene through the Transmission Protection Instrument (TPI),
which allows it to purchase securities issued by member states

whose ﬁnancing conditions have deteriorated for reasons not
justiﬁed by country-speciﬁc fundamentals. However, before
activating it, the Governing Council considers a cumulative set of
criteria to determine whether the countries concerned are pursuing
sound and sustainable ﬁscal and macroeconomic policies. In
practice, this includes compliance with the EU ﬁscal framework,

### Page 3

which would, at a minimum, require the proposed French budget to
be fully adopted.
The ECB has nevertheless left itself some discretion, stating that the
intervention criteria “will be dynamically adjusted to the unfolding
risks and conditions to be addressed”. This brings back memories of
the famous “because it’s France” by former European Commission
President Jean-Claude Juncker. Generally speaking, the ECB will be
in a diﬃcult position: intervening too quickly in support of France

may be viewed unfavourably by other member states and
interpreted as monetary ﬁnancing of the debt of a country
unwilling to follow the rules. That argues strongly against early
intervention. Also, don’t forget that the leadership changes at the
ECB over the next months (Schnabel, Lane, Lagarde) could also
aﬀect how the ECB views TPI.
Given the challenges of justifying the activation of TPI at present, we
expect the ECB to adopt a three-pronged approach to address the

French crisis.
1. The ﬁrst line of defence is a more dovish approach to policy
rates. As bond markets are currently taking over the ECB’s job
of inserting a more restrictive monetary stance on the
economy, there is less need for the ECB to hike rates
aggressively, or to hike rates at all. In fact, the ECB has already
started to prepare for this option, as ECB president Christine
Lagarde recently noted that “while growth has been resilient,
since our last meeting long-term interest rates have risen

notably, which will slow growth and reduce pass-through by
more than projected in our September exercise".
2. The second line of defence would be a covert return to bond
purchases. The ECB could instead pause quantitative tightening
temporarily and reinvest maturing bonds in its portfolio
'ﬂexibly', sending a positive signal to bond markets. This had
already happened during the Covid period.
3. Last but not least, should contagion in eurozone bond markets

intensify, we expect the ECB to eventually trigger the TPI. As
long as France remains non-compliant with the European ﬁscal

rules, the ECB might even consider purchasing bonds from
almost all eurozone countries, except for France.

All in all, next week’s ECB meeting looks set to be uneventful on
action but clearly not on words.

Editorial 
Opportunities from the bond sell-off  
Strategy summary: The global bond sell-off is creating tactical 
and medium-term opportunities. In the US, September’s yield 
surge has improved the risk -reward in 10 -year government 
bonds, presenting an attractive entry point for investors with a 
6-12-month horizon. We believe bond and money markets are 
overly hawkish about the Fed outlook, given disinflation is likely 
to return as impact of oil prices and tariffs ease next year. 
In equities, we stick to quality, especially sectors riding the AI -
led earnings boom. The upcoming Q3 earnings season is likely 
to show AI -investments are powering sectors beyond the 
technology and communications services, such as financials, 
materials and power and electrification. We remain constructive 
on the semiconductor and memory segments. We also expect 
the US Dollar to peak in the next few weeks along with short -
to-medium-term US bond yields. This creates opportunities for 
AUD corporate bonds, Emerging Market USD bonds and gold. 
Attractive risk -reward in US 10 -year government bonds.  
The US 10-year bond yield rose more than 50bps in September 
(biggest monthly rise since 2022) and 115bps this year, amid 
expectations of economic resilience, higher Fed rates, fuel -
driven inflation, AI-related capital demand and fiscal concerns. 
Valuations appear close to fair value, improving the 6-12-month 
risk-reward. Bond arithmetic suggests losses of less than 1% if 
the yield rises to 6%, vs. more than 10% gains if it falls to 4.5%. 
Resilient US economic activity, but softer -than-expected 
inflation. September data showed robust US employment 
(jobless claims, ADP) and core capital-goods orders. However, 
consumer confidence remains depressed, real disposable 
income is flat and households are drawing on savings to sustain 
spending. Against this divided backdrop, core PCE inflation - 
the Fed’s preferred gauge - was softer than expected at 0.2% 
m/m and 3.0% y/y. A revised method to measure PCE inflation 
also indicate that annual core PCE inflation was 35bps lower 
than previously reported, narrowing the gap with CPI inflation. 
Markets appear overly hawkish on the Fed. Money markets 
price about 85bps of tightening over the next 12 months. A 
strong labour market (September’s non -farm payrolls data is 
due tonight) may justify one or two further hikes, but aggressive 
tightening after the sharp rise in bond yields – which has 
already tightened financial conditions - could threaten financial 
stability. US small- and mid-cap equities have already fallen 8–
9% from recent peaks over the past couple of months, 
materially underperforming large caps, as energy and 
borrowing-cost concerns intensify. W hile some Fed speakers 
remain hawkish, we  agree with New York Fed President 
Williams’ that there is no urgency to tighten. We see little 
chance of an October Fed rate hike and expect inflation data 
for Sep-Nov determining whether the Fed moves in December. 
Stay with quality equities and AI beneficiaries.  US large 
caps have absorbed the rise in bond yields relatively well, 
supported by expectations of robust earnings growth led by AI-
related investment. We continue to favour quality companies 
and sectors benefiting from this AI cycle. Semiconductors and 
memory remain preferred exposures: structural AI demand 
should extend the memory upcycle, while long -term supply 
agreements provide better revenue visibility. The opportunity is 
also broadening beyond technology and communication 
services into financials, materials, power and electrification. 
Opportunities in AUD corporate bonds and gold.  The USD 
rebound alongside higher US yields offers a tactical opportunity 
to add higher -yielding Emerging Market USD and non -USD 
bonds. We remain positive on AUD corporate bonds, which 
offer a yield premium over comparable US credit. Our 
constructive medium-term AUD/USD view could add returns for 
USD-based investors over the next 6 –12 months. We would 
also use gold’s recent pullback to add exposure, supported by 
sustained Emerging Market central bank demand. We continue 
to view USD 4,000/oz as important technical support for gold.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions 
  Do you expect more RBA rate hikes after its less hawkish 
guidance? What is your view on the AUD and AUD bonds? 
Our view: We expect the RBA to leave rates unchanged, but 
retain the option to hike again should inflation intensify. We 
maintain our medium-term constructive AUD view and maintain 
our opportunistically bullish AUD corporate bonds idea. 
Rationale: The RBA’s forward guidance remains data dependent, 
explicitly allowing further tightening if needed as inflation remains too 
high.  At 4.60%, Australia’s cash rate is currently 60–85bp above the 
Fed’s 3.75–4.00% range. Thus, even with our two additional 25bp s 
Fed hikes expectation, Australian rates would hold a modest positive 
rate differential. We expect the AUD to strengthen once the Fed 
signals that US rates are near their peak, which should allow resilient 
Australian fundamentals to reassert themselves. Key risks are 
persistently high US yields, weaker China growth or a sharper 
deterioration in global risk sentiment. 
We believe risk-reward has become more attractive for AUD bonds 
following the bear -flattening of the AUD yield curve. Markets are 
already pricing in one more RBA hike through end-2027, presenting 
an attractive opportunity to earn a yield even as increasingly 
restrictive financial conditions reinforc e disinflation over time. 
Meanwhile, the positive interest rate differential with the US supports 
the currency outlook, supporting the case for AUD bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  What does the incoming US data tell us about the path for 
US 10-year bond yields from here? 
Our view: The risk -reward in US 10 -year yields has turned 
attractive following their move higher amid incoming US data. 
Rationale: Recent US data has indeed started to show softer-than-
expected inflation. However, market technicals are likely temporarily 
delaying a move lower in bond yields, with reports suggesting the  
rise in yields is creating forced selling amongst hedge funds and real 
estate investment trusts. Nevertheless, we do not expect this to be 
an extended selloff. We have initiated an opportunistic bullish idea 
on US 10-year government bonds this week and continue to believe 
this idea offers an attractive risk/reward on a 6-12-month horizon as 
yields become attractive enough to entice buyer flow to return. On a 
broader allocation basis, we retain our structural preference for 3-7-
year maturities in bonds. 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

Australia-US interest rate differentials and positive 
outlook for the AUD

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in the GBP ahead of the UK 
budget announcement next month? 
Our view: We maintain a medium-term constructive GBP view, 
but keep near-term exposure measured ahead of the 28 October 
Budget. We would view any pre -Budget weakness as an 
opportunity to gradually build GBP exposure. 
Rationale: The government’s decision to adjust the pension triple 
lock from 2030 is an early indication of greater willingness to tackle 
longer-term spending pressures, reinforcing Chancellor Healey’s 
commitment to fiscal discipline and meeting the fiscal rules. Markets 
are likely to scrutinise the Budget closely for evidence that this 
discipline extends to near -term tax and spending decisions. A 
credible Budget should help contain pressure on UK government 
bond yields and support GBP. Any fiscal slippage remains a key risk.  
Near term, we prefer measured GBP exposure as elevated US yields 
and repricing towards further Fed tightening could continue to 
support the USD. Over the medium term, we expect GBP to rebound 
as the Fed eventually signals the peak of its tightening cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  How would different US mid-term election outcome 
scenarios affect the outlook for US equities? 
Our view: We believe election outcomes will have a limited 
impact over the next 12 months. We remain positive on US 
equities driven by fundamental earnings growth.  
Rationale: Over the past 50 years, the ruling US President’s party 
has retained control of both chambers of Congress on only two 
occasions (1978 and 2002). All other elections result ed in an 
opposition sweep or a divided government.  
Based on the latest prediction markets, it appears that an opposition 
sweep (both the House and Senate flips from Republican to 
Democrats) is more likely than a divided government ( in which the 
House flips to Democrats , while the Senate remains under 
Republican control). A scenario where Republicans retain a majority 
is least likely per prediction markets. 
While mid -term elections may introduce near -term volatility, we 
caution against overreaction. Historically, the average return for the 
S&P 500 following an opposition sweep is 17% over the next 12 
months, but the average return following a divided government is not 
significantly worse, at 15%. We prefer to base our investment views 
on fundamental earnings growth, where investor attention should 
focus on the upcoming Q3 earnings season. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst

opportunity to gradually build GBP exposure over

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is the outlook for  the memory chip and broader 
semiconductors industry? 
Our view: We remain positive on semiconductors and memory 
chips. Structural AI demand should prolong the memory 
upcycle while long-term agreements improve revenue visibility. 
Rationale: Recent industry commentary points to tighter memory 
supply-demand conditions through 2028, reinforcing our view that AI 
demand is likely to extend the memory upcycle. Furthermore, adoption 
of long-term agreements improves revenue and earnings visibility. 
Despite debates around “Pacing the Frontier”, we have seen a flurry 
of new model releases, along with the introduction of new consumer 
AI agents capable of comparing prices online and making 
purchases. These are early days, but the path is open for a business-
to-agent (B2A) economy where businesses increasingly interact with 
AI assistants acting on customers’ behalf. As these applications are 
increasingly inference intensive, wider adoption should support 
demand for memory, with its share of global semiconductor 
revenues projected to rise from 30.7% in 2025 to 66.7% in 2027. 
Against this backdrop, we maintain our Opportunistic idea on Global 
Semiconductors. Given memory  chips’ cyclical nature, investors 
should avoid overconcentration in memory names and maintain a 
diversified exposure across Big Tech and semiconductors. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Does a US bond yield stress test raise the risk to your 
constructive view on US equities? 
Our view: We have a target for the S&P 500 index of 8400 over 
the next 12 months. A scenario of a sustained rise in the US 10-
year government bond yield to 5.6% could lead to negative 
returns from the current S&P500 level (7,666). 
Rationale: Our base case 12-month target for the S&P 500 is 8,400, 
which assumes 2027 S&P 500 EPS of USD 420 and a 20x forward 
P/E multiple. A rise in bond yields would have an impact on equity 
valuations, as a higher discount rate reduces the present value of 
future earnings. W e stress -test our target under different 10 -year 
yield and earnings scenarios.  
A rise in bond yields would arguably be associated with a stronger 
growth environment and hence, even higher earnings. However, 
assuming unchanged EPS at our base case of USD 420, a 
(sustained) rise in the 10 -year bond yield to 5.6% would lead to 
negative returns from the current level  if not offset by a further 
improvement in earnings growth . However, in o ur base case  we 
expect the 10-year yield is more likely to fall from here. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist

## Document 454: ここが知りたい『投資信託販売チャネルの変化と現在地』

Source PDF: all report pdf/ここが知りたい『投資信託販売チャネルの変化と現在地』.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

投資信託販売チャネルの変化と現在地

資産運用調査部　主席研究員　大木 剛（おおき つよし）
　個人の資産運用において、 投資信託はNISAによる資
産形成やリタイアメン ト資金の運用などで、 重要性を増し
ている。 投資信託の販売では、 1998年12月の銀行窓販
解禁から四半世紀以上が経過した。 ここでは、 1999年以
降の公募投資信託の販売チャネル別の純資産額 （販売 ・
解約 ・ 時価変動が反映） と構成比率の推移を資料 １ で確認
したい。
　銀行窓販の解禁から1年後の1999年末時点で、 証券
は46.4兆円 （比率90％） 、 銀行等は2.8兆円 （同5％） とな
り、 その後も銀行等は預金取引等を背景とする顧客基盤
を活かし、 2007年末には34.0兆円 （同43％） まで比率
を高めた。 一方、 証券は45.3兆円 （同57％） と比率が低
下した。
　もっ とも、 銀行等の比率は2007～2008年をピークに
低下し、 証券の比率は上昇に転じた。 証券の純資産額に
は機関投資家も保有するETF （上場投資信託） が含まれ、
2010年代以降は日本銀行による大量買入れも統計上の
証券比率を大きく押し上げた。 資料 １ ・ ２ では、 個人向け投
資信託の動きをみるためETFを除いており、 それでも
2026年8月末時点では、 証券は159.9兆円 （同70％） 、 銀
行等は67.2兆円 （同29％） となっている。
投資信託の銀行窓販は証券にどれだけ迫ったか
　2009年以降の証券の比率上昇には、 証券各社の取組
みに加え、 いくつかの要因が考えられる。
　まず、 近年はNISAなどを活用した投資信託積立の拡大
により、 資産形成層を中心に大手オンライン証券会社の
利用が大きく増えている。
　また、 メガバンク等では、 顧客層に応じたグループ内の
銀行と証券の役割分担を強化し、 銀行顧客の富裕層や資
産形成層にもニーズに応じて、 証券がサービスを提供す
る動きが寄与している。
　さらに地域銀行において、 銀行の個人向け投資信託事
業を大手証券等との包括提携の下で共同運営する動き
も、 証券比率の上昇に作用している。
　一方で、 銀行等の純資産額も近年は堅調に伸びてお
り、 比率低下は、 銀行の落ち込みによるものではなく、 証
券の伸びが上回った影響が大きい。
　もっ とも、 「銀行と証券のどちらが高成長か」 という比較
より、 顧客ニーズに応じた役割分担の重要性が増してい
る。 2026年7月に政府が策定した 「金融戦略」 も、 インベ
ス トメン トチェーンの機能強化を掲げている。 多様な資産
運用ニーズに各金融サービスの担い手が強みを活かし
て応えることが、 資産運用立国の深化につながろう。
資産運用立国の深化に求められる取組み
資料1 公募投資信託 販売チャネル別純資産額推移
（注） 直販は資産運用会社が直接販売する形態。 証券はETFを除く
（出所） 資産運用業協会資料より、 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

## Document 504: 中国经济运行平稳9月略有回升

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国经济运行平稳9月略有回升.pdf
Report source: 第一创业
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告
证券研究报告 请务必阅读正文后免责条款部分
宏观研究 2026年10月19日
第 1 页 共 7 页
中国经济运行平稳 9 月略有回升
[Table_Summary]
核心观点：
三季度 GDP同比增长 4.5%，较二季度回升 0.2个百分点，高于 4.4%的 Wind预期。
而从三季度季节调整后的环比看，三季度增长 1.2%，较二季度回升 0.3个百分点，
较去年同期回升 0.1个百分点。分产业看，第三产业增加值同比增长 3.6%，较二季
度回落 0.1个百分点；第二产业增长 3.4%，较二季度回升 0.4个百分点；第三产业
增长 5.2%，较二季度回升 0.1个百分点。
从全国规模以上工业增加值看，9 月同比实际增速为 5.5%，与 Wind预期持平，较 8
月回升 0.3个百分点；从累计增速看，前三季度为 5.3%，较上半年回落 0.1 个百分
点。从工业产能利用率看，三季度为 73.1%，较二季度回升 0.1个百分点；从工业
企业产销率看，9 月为 97%，较 8月回升 0.2个百分点；而前三季度为 96.4%，较上

半年回升 0.6个百分点。从出口交货值同比看，9 月为 17%，创 2021年 5 月以来新
高，较 8 月回升 5.9 个百分点；而前三季度为 10.7%，较上半年回升 1 个百分点。
前三季度中国经济的平稳运行，主要还是得益于出口的强力拉动，而从内需看相对
较弱。
社会消费品零售总额的名义增速看，9 月同比为 1.2%，高于 Wind 预期 0.7%，较 8
月回升 0.7个百分比；而前三季度为 1.1%，较上半年回落 0.2个百分点；从服务业
生产指数的实际增速看，9 月同比为 4.8%，较 8 月回升 0.7个百分点；而前三季度
为 4.7%，较上半年回落 0.1个百分点。
从固定资产投资的累计增速看，前三季度季度同比为-7.2%，与 1-8 月持平，但较
上半年回落 1.5个百分点。其中，制造业为 2.1%，较 1-8月回落 0.2个百分点；基
建为-6.1%，较 1-8 月回落 0.7 个百分点；而房地产投资累计同比增速为-20.6%，
较 1-8月回落 0.7个百分点。
风险提示：

对上述事件发展趋势的点评，存在由于经济增长、行业竞争、外部经济环境和政策
等变化，而不如预期的可能。
第一创业证券研究所
分析师：李怀军
证书编号：S1080510120001

### Page 2

宏观经济
证券研究报告 请务必阅读正文后免责条款部分
第 2 页 共 7 页
一、2026 年三季度 GDP 同比与季调环比均较二季度略有回升
2026 年 9 月 19 日国家统计局公布 2026 年三季度宏观经济数据，按可比价格计算，
三季度 GDP 同比增长 4.5%，较二季度回升 0.2 个百分点，高于 4.4%的市场预期（Wind）。
而从三季度季节调整后的环比看，三季度增长 1.2%，较二季度回升 0.3 个百分点，较去
年同期回升 0.1 个百分点（图 1 所示）。初步核算，前三季度国内生产总值 107 万亿元，
按不变价格计算，同比增长 4.6%，较上半年回落 0.1 个百分点。
图 1 2026年三季度 GDP同比和季调环比均较二季度略有回升
资料来源：国家统计局，第一创业证券研究所整理
分产业看，2026 年三季度第一产业增加值同比增长 3.6%，较二季度回落 0.1 个百分
点；第二产业增加值同比增长 3.4%，较二季度回升 0.4 个百分点；第三产业增加值同比
增长 5.2%，较二季度回升 0.1 个百分点。

从 9 月全国规模以上工业增加值看（表 1 所示），9 月同比增速为 5.5%，与市场预
期（Wind）持平，但较 8 月回升 0.3 个百分点；9 月季调环比为 0.61%，较 8 月 0.54%略
有回升；从累计增速看，前三季度工业增加值同比增速为 5.3%，较上半年回落 0.1 个百
分点。
从工业产能利用率看，三季度为 73.1%，较二季度回升 0.1 个百分点；从累计值看，
前三季度季度为 73.2%，较上半年回落 0.1 个百分点。从工业企业产销率看，9 月为 97%，
较 8 月回升 0.2 个百分点；而从累计值看，前三季度为 96.4%，较上半年回升 0.6 个百
分点。从出口交货值同比看，9月为 17%，创 2021 年 5 月以来的新高，较 8 月回升 5.9 个百分
点；而从累计值看，前三季度为 10.7%，较上半年回升 1 个百分点。因此，前三季度中国经济
运行平稳，主要还是得益于出口的强力拉动，而从内需看相对较弱，但 9 月有所回升。

半年高 0.8 个百分点。9 月贸易顺差为 1335亿美元，创历史新高；从累计值看，前三季度累计
贸易顺差为 9381亿美元，较去年同期的 8704亿美元高 238亿美元，同比增长 7.8%。
二、三季度生产仍主要依靠外需拉动经济内生动力需进一步加强
从总体上看，2026年 9 月中国经济总体呈现温和触底态势，较 8 月见底回升明显，但前三
季度经济数据较上半年是有所回落的。从经济结构上看，上半年生产主要还是依靠外需拉动，
经济内生动力不足，国内需求的潜力仍需进一步释放，以做强国内大循环、特别是消费的潜力
仍需进一步释放。
首先，从城乡居民收入同比增速看，前三季度全国居民人均可支配收入 34230 元，同比
名义增长 5.3%，扣除价格因素实际增长 4.4%，比同期 GDP增速低 0.2个百分点。其中，城镇居
民人均可支配收入 44945 元，同比名义增长 4.5%，实际增长 3.6%；农村居民人均可支配收入
18809 元，同比名义增长 6.3%，实际增长 5.6%。从收入来源看，全国居民人均工资性收入、经

营净收入、财产净收入、转移净收入分别名义增长 5.3%、6.6%、1.6%、5.7%，说明居民财产性
收入的增速显著低于其它收入来源，随着中国进入中等发达国家的行列，资本市场在国民经济
中的地位仍需进一步提高。
从城镇居民支出同比增速看，三季度人均消费支出 27312元，比上年同期名义增长 3%，扣
除价格因素影响，实际增长 2.1%，远低于同期 4.6%GDP实际增速，也低于同期 3.6%的人均可支
配收入实际增速（图 2 所示）。有效需求不足也反映在失业率上，虽然 9 月全国城镇调查失业
率为 5.2%，较 8 月回落 0.1个百分点，但较 6 月回升 0.2 个百分点。从结构上看，年青人的失
业率仍然较高，特别是每年近千万的高校毕业生的就业压力较大。
图 2 2026 年前三季度城镇居民人均消费支出实际增速远低于可支配收入与 GDP

其次，从下游消费结构看，三季度服务业消费要远强于零售（表 1 所示），零售中非耐用
品消费要强于耐用品（图 3 所示），这主要是因为去年“以旧换新”政策对耐用消费品消费的
提振，但这种刺激的短期作用明显，长期则形成拖累，今年三前季度耐用消费品零售增速的回
落就是明证。
图 3 2026年前三季度各行业零售名义同比增速与上半年的对比
资料来源：国家统计局，第一创业证券整理
从零售增速上看，前三季度限额以上社会消费品零售总额名义增速排名前五的行业包括：
通讯器材类（18.1%）、烟酒类（12.6%）、粮油食品类（6.8%）、文化办公用品类（6.2%）、
化妆品类（6.1%）和饮料类（5.4%），只有这 6个行业的同比增速均在 5%以上，而这些行业均
为必需消费品行业；而汽车类（-14.9%）、建筑及装潢材料类（-9.7%）、石油及制品类（-5.5%）、
家用电器和音像器材类（-4.9%）、家具类（-4.7%）、体育娱乐用品类（-3.4）和金银珠宝类

（-2.4%），这些行业的增速均在零及以下，反映出城乡居民在 2026 年对于与房地产和汽车相
关的大额耐用消费、以及可选消费的谨慎态度。与上半年相比，两极分化更为明显，排名第一
和排名最后的行业增速差距扩大了 6 个百分点。
最后，房地产对经济的拖累仍十分明显。前三季度房屋新开工同比增速为-24.9%，仅较 1-8
月回落 0.1 个百分点，但较上半年回落 1.5个百分点；房屋施工面积同比增速为-12.8%，与 1-8
月持平，但较上半年回落 0.3 个百分点；竣工面积同比增速为-25.1%，较 1-8月和上半年均回
落 1.4 个百分点；房屋销售面积同比增速为-13.2%，较 1-8月回落 1.1个百分点，较上半年回
落 1.6 个百分点。

从房地产价格看，9 月 70 个大中城市新建商品住宅价格指数同比为-3%，较 8 月回升 0.3
个百分比；9 月二手住宅价格指数同比为-4.8%，也较 8 月回升 0.3 个百分点。就环比而言，9
月 70 个大中城市新建商品住宅价格指数环比为-0.1%，较 8 月回升 0.1 个百分点；9 月二手住
宅价格指数环比为-0.3%，与 8 月持平。
因此，从总体上看，9 月房地产景气度回落速度明显放缓，价格、特别是新房价格上有企

稳迹象，但能否真正筑底回升，仍需进一步观察。

摘要：
当地时间 21 ⽇，美国总统特使威特科夫在圣彼得堡与俄总统普京⻓谈约 3 ⼩时后，俄总统助理乌
沙科夫通报称，普京愿意重启俄美乌三⽅谈判，但他淡化了达成停战协议的可能性，称乌克兰
和欧洲提出的新和平⽅案缺乏 “ 实质内容 ” 。英国《卫报》称，尽管俄罗斯对和平⽅案的反应冷
淡，美国总统特朗普仍声称这是 “ 我们最接近达成协议的⼀次 ” 。乌总统泽连斯基同⽇称，克⾥姆
林宫释放出的信号是 “ 积极的 ” ，但对俄是否将会晤表⽰担忧。
俄⽅：对解决冲突有实际帮助的提议并不多
据《俄罗斯报》报道，乌沙科夫 21 ⽇通报称，俄美会谈内容详细且坦诚，乌克兰问题是会晤核
⼼。威特科夫向普京介绍了欧洲和乌克兰官员在挪威会谈期间提出的解决冲突的新⽅案。但俄

罗斯⽅⾯认为，其中对解决冲突有实际帮助的提议并不多。普京告知威特科夫，俄⽅准备于 11
⽉中旬重启俄美乌三⽅会谈，会谈主题涉及冲突结束后俄乌安全体系建设。
乌沙科夫表⽰，普京向威特科夫阐述了俄⽅对前线局势的评估。普京称，俄罗斯军队在前线推
进，掌握了战略主动权。普京和威特科夫还谈到了有关俄罗斯向美国和全球市场供应柴油协议
的执⾏问题。乌沙科夫说， “ 双⽅强调，该协议不仅符合俄美双⽅利益，还将有助于稳定全球燃
料市场 ” 。
据俄新社报道，俄总统新闻秘书佩斯科夫 21 ⽇告诉记者： “ 我们看到特朗普真诚且始终如⼀地希
望促成和平解决，并使⼀切朝着和平解决的⽅向发展。 ” 他同时表⽰，⽬前尚未就美国特使下次访
俄达成任何协议。据俄罗斯卫星通讯社报道，挪威⾸相斯特勒告诉挪威⼴播公司，威特科夫与
普京会晤后将返回奥斯陆，继续就乌克兰问题进⾏磋商。
特朗普：现在是我们最接近达成协议的时候
⽩宫 21 ⽇表⽰，美国与俄罗斯正朝着解决俄乌冲突的⽅向迈进。特朗普当天说，前⼏天和普京
谈到了解决俄乌冲突的问题，“ 也许现在是我们最接近达成协议的时候 ” 。特朗普认为，鉴于谈

判正处于微妙阶段，现在不值得加⼤对莫斯科的压⼒。
据 RBC- 乌克兰通讯社报道，泽连斯基 21 ⽇称，威特科夫与普京的会晤具有重要意义。他表⽰，
俄美乌三⽅应尽快会⾯，开始实质性⼯作。
不过，据英国⼴播公司 22 ⽇报道，乌克兰及其欧洲盟友对普京的说法提出质疑，他们称前线实
际上已陷⼊僵局。据乌克兰国际⽂传电讯社报道，泽连斯基 21 ⽇在接受美国福克斯新闻频道直
播采访时称，愿在保留⾄少 75% 领⼟控制权的基础上达成和平协议。他将接受⼀项按当前战线
冻结战争的协议。乌⽅随后还将寻求美国总统和欧洲盟友的安全保障， “ 以确保和平与安全 ” 。
未来前景扑朔迷离
俄乌冲突仍在持续，未来前景扑朔迷离。据俄《⽣意⼈报》 22 ⽇报道，俄罗斯国防部通报，当
天凌晨，俄军对乌境内军⼯设施、交通基础设施和港⼝等⽬标实施了⼤规模打击。

## Document 525: 債券市場總評：投等債吸引力增

Report source: Fubon
Date: 2026-10-20

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

台北富邦銀行 
Oct 20，2026 | 2:00 PM (GMT+8) 
債券市場總評： 投等債吸引力增
投資研究部 
 Research 
Financial Market 
▎通膨壓力續高，年底升息預期仍存 
10 月上旬以來，美債殖利率自高檔回落，高殖利率逐
步吸引買盤，股市震盪亦帶動避險資金回流債市。不
過，受燃料與能源價格上揚帶動，9 月 CPI 年增率由
3.4%升至 3.7%，PPI 年增率亦由 5.6%升至 6.3%，核
心 CPI 與核心 PPI 年增幅也高於預期，顯示物價壓力
仍高。加上零售銷售月增 0.8%優於預期、初領失業金
人數維持低位，反映消費與勞動市場仍具韌性，市場
因此雖傾向 Fed 本月先按兵不動，但 12 月再升息 1
碼的預期仍存。 
圖 1  9 月 CPI、PPI 年增率同步走高

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/19 
▎避險買盤增溫 ， 殖利率回落、債市修復 
近期美債買盤回升也反映在公債標售結果，3 年、10
年及 30 年期標售需求皆表現穩健或強勁，提供長端美
債支撐。雖然中東情勢反覆使油價維持高檔，通膨預
期仍偏高，但 Fed 官員多傾向先觀察，緩和短期升息
壓力。此外，高殖利率環境使 AI 與科技股估值面臨修
正壓力，股市震盪帶動避險資金流入美債，使 10 年期
美債殖利率自 10 月初 5.30%高位顯著回降至低點
5.04%後反彈。

圖 2 公債與投等債反彈較強，惟油價仍處高檔

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/19 
◼ 9 月 CPI、PPI 升溫，消費與就業具韌性，年底升息預期仍存。不過，美債標售穩
健、Fed 官員偏向不急於在 10 月升息，股市震盪帶動避險買盤，10 年期殖利率
回降後反彈，債市自低檔修復，近期美國公債與投等債反彈幅度高於非投等債。 
◼ 近期美國投等債利差反彈相對受控，Q4「淨淨」發行量可望逐月下降，年底季節
性亦有利，技術面改善，企業獲利強勁，信用面穩健。美投等債殖利率仍處高檔，
高票息提供報酬緩衝，歷史經驗顯示，高殖利率通常伴隨較佳未來 12 個月報酬。 
◼ 產業配置上，金融債存續期間較短、利率敏感度較低，加上銀行貸款與存款維持
成長，資產品質穩健，防禦性相對較佳。科技債殖利率與OAS 高於整體投等債市
場，反映 AI 資本支出擴張推升發債供給，但科技企業財務體質仍佳，利差走寬後
收益補償提升。整體投資可偏向中短天期，依風險承受度與收益需求擇優布局。

▎信用利差走寬，美投等債相對具韌性 
近期債市明顯承壓，全球公債殖利率一度快速上行、利

之走寬。儘管近期美國投等債與非投等債 OAS 自低位
有所回升，但若以近 10 年歷史區間比較，目前仍處於
相對低檔。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/14

▎Q4 淨供給緩和 ， 投等債利差有望收斂 
企業債發行量維持高檔，仍對債市形成一定壓力，但進
入 Q4 後，美國投等債市場的供給壓力可望逐步緩和。
若進一步觀察「淨淨」發行量（net-net issuance ），
即扣除到期本金、贖回款與票息等回流資金後，市場實
際需消化的新增公司債供給，根據 J.P. Morgan 預估，
Q4「淨淨」 發行量將逐月下降 ， 新增供給對利差的壓力
可望減輕。

圖 5  Q4 美國投等債淨淨供給量預估逐月下降

資料來源：JPM，2026/10/2

同時，過去 4 年平均來看，美國投等債利差在 11 月、 
12 月偏向收斂，年底季節性有助支撐利差表現。供給
壓力下降與年底季節性相互配合 ， 使第4 季美國投等債
利差具備較有利的技術面條件。 
圖 6 近 4 年 11 月~12 月美國投等債利差呈現收窄

註：本圖採用 J.P. Morgan 美國投等債指數(JULI)。負值代表月末利
差較月初收窄，正值代表利差擴大。 
資料來源：JPM，2026/10/2

▎企業獲利預估強勁，支撐信用基本面 
近期殖利率上行雖受到通膨與貨幣政策預期影響 ， 部分
也反映美國經濟與企業獲利仍具韌性 。AI 基礎建設需求
與大型科技企業資本支出持續擴張 ， 加上企業利潤率維
持高檔 ， 成為獲利成長的重要支撐 。 標普500 企業獲利
自 2026 年以來明顯加速 ， 第1 季與第 2 季分別年成長
29%與 52%，Q3 與 Q4 預估仍達 30%與 28%。獲利
動能延續 ， 有助維持投等債發行公司的償債能力與財務
彈性，支撐高利率環境下的信用韌性。

圖 7 標普 500 企業 Q3、Q4 獲利年增率將維持高成長

資料來源：JPM，2026/10/2

▎高殖利率提供報酬緩衝 
目前美國投等債殖利率仍處相對高檔 ， 較高的殖利率可
提高票息緩衝，也為後續報酬提供較有利的起點。根據
JPM 自 1990 年以來的歷史資料顯示 ， 美國投等債當期
殖利率與未來 12 個月總報酬呈正向關係，殖利率較高
時，後續 12 個月報酬通常也較佳。在利率仍可能波動
的環境下，較高票息有助吸收部分價格波動，使目前的
收益條件較過去低殖利率時期更具支撐。 
圖 8 高殖利率通常伴隨較佳 12 個月報酬

資料來源：JPM，2026/10/12 
▎金融偏防禦，科技債補償提升 
綜合信用基本面、供需條件與收益水準，美國投等債整
體展望仍偏正向。在產業方面，金融債在殖利率接近整
體投等債市場的情況下，存續期間明顯較短。近期大型
銀行財報亦顯示，貸款與存款維持成長，整體資產品質
仍穩健，為金融債基本面提供支撐。科技債殖利率與
OAS 則高於整體市場，主要反映 AI 資本支出擴張帶動
發債供給快速增加。儘管融資需求升高，大型科技企業
整體資產負債表仍相對穩健 ， 利差走寬後的收益補償也
有所提升 。 整體配置上 ， 在利率仍具不確定性的環境下 ，
可偏向中短天期， 並依風險承受度與收益需求擇優布局 。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/15

## Document 562: 凱基投顧_Takeaway_7947 補丁科_沈漢軒_20261012

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_Takeaway_7947 補丁科_沈漢軒_20261012.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 1

訪談重點摘要

補丁科 
(7947 TT, NT$881, 未評等)

焦點內容 
1. 記憶體牆為 AI 效能的主要瓶頸，其中推論對延遲與能耗的要求高於訓
練。硬體算力約每 2 年成長 3 倍，DRAM 頻寬卻只有約 1.6 倍，而
GPU+HBM 採用的 2.5D 封裝，延遲與功耗也相對較高。公司以 3D 堆
疊 near memory 架構切入，有望兼顧接近 SRAM 的延遲，以及遠高於
SRAM 的容量，適合需同時服務百萬級使用者的推論應用。  
2. 公司在 2016 年首次投片即成功，4Gb 產品頻寬達 144GB/s、延遲僅
17ns (HBM 約 40-50ns)，並於 2017 年在 ISSCC 發表。 3D WoW 
hybrid bonding 並非公司獨有，真正的門檻在於客戶、晶圓大廠、南亞
科與公司需四方深度共同開發，公司負責記憶體架構與介面的協同設
計，並以 KGD 技術確保堆疊良率。南亞科持股約 35%，HiBaLL -M 多
層系列目前為 4 層，客戶已要求 8 層。 
3. AI 產品營收占比由 2024 年的 4%提升至 1H26 的約 40%，3Q26 營收
也較 2Q26 成長約 43%。公司自 2025 年起簽訂 SOW，預期明年有客
戶進入量產並開始貢獻營收，其中部分客戶將採 turnkey 模式，量產規
模放大後獲利率有望提高。公司預期 2028-29 年量持續增加， 2 至 3
年內將產生重大影響。  
結論 
補丁科以自有高頻寬低延遲架構結合 3D 堆疊 DRAM，受惠於 AI 推論對低
延遲、低功耗與大容量的需求，以及雲端客戶走向客製化記憶體的趨勢。
短期營收來自 AI 客製化專案與傳統 DRAM 業務；中期成長動能 為明年起
客戶陸續進入量產，帶動客製化專案由設計階段轉向量產階段；長期則有
機會由雲端延伸至邊緣推論，並隨客戶增加與規格收斂推出通用化產品。

營運現況 
 營收：3Q26 季增約 43%，過去六季趨勢向上  
 EPS：1H26 9.35 元，1H26 營收與獲利同步成長  
 產品組合 
 AI 產品營收占比由 2024 年的 4%提升至 1H26 約 40% 
 傳統 DRAM 業務占 1H26 營收約 60%，提供 SoC 廠與系統廠，
並協助客戶客製化  
營運展望 
 明年預期有客戶進入量產並開始貢獻營收，2028 -29 年量持續增加  
 AI 占比持續提升，預期 2至 3年內產生重大影響  
 傳統產品除維持現況外成長有限，AI 為未來重心  
 客戶表示已取得 CSP 大廠訂單，公司目前開發未來產品，產品驗證
成功即可進入後續導入，有別於過去先做產品再推市場

凱基投顧

沈漢軒

重要免責聲明，詳見最終頁

### Page 2

台灣 補丁科

十月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 2

 公司為無廠房的 IP 與設計公司，生產由晶圓大廠 (邏輯 )與南亞科
(DRAM)負責，由客戶向其下單 
成長動能 
 AI 記憶體：記憶體牆與 3D 堆疊解決方案  
 硬體算力約每 2 年成長 3倍，DRAM 頻寬約每 2 年成長 1.6 倍，
二十年累積形成落差  
 現行 GPU+HBM 採 2.5D 封裝，匯流排較長，存取延遲較高、負
載與功耗較高，且台積電 CoWoS 產能大部分由 NVIDIA/AMD 使
用 
 公司採 3D 堆疊 near memory 架構：DRAM 先堆疊，再以 3D 方
式與 CPU/GPU 堆疊 
 HBM 採 micro -bump，公司採 3D WoW hybrid bonding ，pitch
小一個數量級以上，頻寬等於 IO 數乘以頻率，IO 數增加可大幅
提升頻寬，同時達成高頻寬 /低延遲/低功耗 
 AI 推論需求 
 訓練端 HBM 容量遠大於 SRAM，對延遲容忍度尚可；推論端延
遲非常關鍵，已有業者不採 HBM、改採 SRAM 
 客戶以同一模型比較 GPU+HBM 與自家 SRAM 平台，SRAM 平台
約十幾二十秒出答案，GPU+HBM 明顯較慢，使用者無法接受  
 SRAM 容量在百萬級同時使用者的情境下遠遠不足； 3D 堆疊
DRAM 容量遠高於 SRAM，延遲與能耗可做到非常接近 SRAM 
 單一項目未必勝過 SRAM，但整體 AI 效能優於 HBM 與 SRAM 
 HBM 因容量大仍有其價值，公司目標為超越 HBM，產品目前更
適合推論應用  
 技術累積與驗證  
 2014 年與 Intel 合作高頻寬低延遲記憶體計畫，2016 年投片一
次成功，容量 4Gb，頻寬 144GB/s，高於當時 HBM2 
 延遲 17ns，HBM2 乃至今日 HBM 約 40-50ns，成果 2017 年 2
月於 ISSCC 發表 
 2017 年起累積高頻寬低延遲架構，持續為不同客戶客製化，並
具備多種 interface 設計經驗 
 專利全部自行開發，部分已核准，部分申請中  
 競爭優勢 
 邏輯設計能力是 3D 堆疊關鍵：單片良率 80%，不處理的情況下
堆疊 4片剩約 41%，8片剩約 16.7%，10 片剩約 11%，無法量
產，須在底層邏輯設計端克服 
 3D 堆疊接合前可測，接合後 (含溫度循環等熱應力後) 仍可測並可
修復，客戶系統端亦可再測再修復，測試設計須整合進邏輯，與
客戶如同一家公司般共同設計

### Page 3

台灣 補丁科

十月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 3

 與南亞科策略合作，針對客戶高頻寬需求在技術上突破原有框架  
 3D 堆疊技術本身並非稀缺，包括華邦/ 力積電/聯電/台積電/南亞
科均具備，南亞科屏東廠將投入 3D 堆疊與測試 
 差異化在於客製化共同設計能力與團隊 20 多年合作累積的首次
量產成功經驗  
 商業模式與合作條件  
 過去 DRAM 依 JEDEC 標準，各家依標準自行設計；現今 AI 客戶
為超車走客製化，公司從 interface/ 規格定義起即與客戶共同開
發，設計範圍並含處理單元 
 客戶皆為具備足夠規模與資本的 AI 雲端客戶，需使用最先進邏
輯製程(全球目前僅台積電能提供) ，並需 DRAM 端夥伴 
 三大記憶體廠標準品量大獲利高，目前不願大量提供客製化；中
國有其限制，南亞科 DRAM 製程為台灣最先進，現有大客戶都
想找南亞科，也是南亞科積極擴產的原因之一  
 公司角色為與客戶/ 台積電 /南亞科三方共同開發，並協同客戶設
計介面 
 與南亞科的關係  
 2010 年南亞科持股超過 50%，2017 年協議結束後仍維持晶圓
代工合作； 2024 年起重新討論，2025 年因應 AI 記憶體客製
化需求再度投資，目前為最大股東，持股約 35% 
 維持晶圓代工關係，合作模式彈性，可深化合作也可各自發展  
 產品線 
 以 HBLL-RAM 架構為基礎，目前幾乎所有研發量均投入 AI 相關
產品，2017 年架構早期以 2D 方案提供低成本選擇  
 HiBaLL -M 多層系列：多顆 DRAM 以 TSV 堆疊，目前為 4 層，客
戶要求 8層，未來視技術進步而定  
 HiBaLL -S 單層系列：僅一層，相同條件下可提供 10 倍以上頻寬
優勢 
 頻寬>30TBps ，延遲<15ns ，能耗 約 0.2pJ/bit ，HBM 為 2–
3pJ/bit 
 有客戶需求達多個 PB 等級的容量，且要求更低能耗  
 兩者取捨取決於客戶規格需求，並非哪一種必然勝出 
 應用方向 
 目前主要已啟動的 開發案件皆為雲端客戶，為其下一世代擴大服
務做準備 
 邊緣推論客戶已開始洽談，進度較慢屬 AI 應用由雲端先行再落
地邊緣的需求節奏，並非技術問題  
 標準方案無法滿足、對功耗/ 成本有特殊要求的邊緣客戶，也會
來找公司客製化

### Page 4

台灣 補丁科

十月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 4

 傳統 DRAM 業務 
 過去 20 年累積的產品仍在量產，因無自有工廠，以授權及客戶
關係為主 
QA 
 客製化需求增加，是否代表 DRAM 景氣波動減小、價格較為穩定？  
 是，這也是南亞科約 2 至 3 年前決定投入 AI 客製化 DRAM 的原
因，因量體不及三大廠，需走差異化  
 現階段先挑選優質且能長期合作、降低波動性的客戶，客戶成功
則公司與南亞科即成功  
 客戶目標是超越 HBM，通用化並非優點；未來客戶增加、規格
收斂後，有機會推出較通用化的產品  
 與華邦的 CUBE 有何差異？ 
 對方先推出自有標準再尋找客戶導入，並尋求外部協助做標準化
與介面，客製化為輔  
 公司以客戶需求出發，客戶千挑萬選後找來，是希望在製程與設
計上取得特別的東西  
 與愛普的技術路線有何差異？ 
 兩家各有 DRAM 架構，公司認為自家架構更具優勢  
 測試方面， server 等級良率必須測試與修復，公司做法與對方不
同 
 公司深入客戶環境，與客戶緊密共同設計  
 愛普營收規模大於公司，部分來自公司未涉足的業務，目前 AI
需求強勁，各家隨客戶進展成長 
 NVIDIA 若自行在 GPU 上堆疊 SRAM，公司產品是否仍有必要？  
 HBM4 以上也在考慮 3D，NV 可能把部分功能整合進 HBM 的
base die，並與 DRAM 廠討論 3D 整合 
 採客製化或標準化尚不確定，時程比公司稍慢  
 NVIDIA 偏通用型，需兼顧所有應用，CSP 大廠也希望有更多選
擇 
 即使 NVIDIA 做客製化，全球客製化需求也不會只有一家，公司
與南亞科的組合具競爭力  
 3D 堆疊在處理器上，散熱是否會成為更大的問題，是否存在功耗
上限？ 
 功耗是 AI 共同指標，設計初期即規劃，也是客戶採購重要標準  
 公司產品功耗至少為 HBM 的四分之一，目前合作的客戶，整體
系統設計尚未看到散熱成為此方案的限制，這類問題可以解決  
 目前為 4層，客戶要求 8層，設計須隨層數調整

### Page 5

台灣 補丁科

十月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 5

 良率與修復如何看待？  
 設計本身有面積/ 測試等 overhead，4層/8層的目標需分別設定  
 客戶要求目標為 95%，目前至少可做到 80%，有機會更高 
 DRAM 層與層之間也是用 hybrid bonding ？是否提供 2.5D 方案？ 
 DRAM 與 SoC 之間採 hybrid bonding ，多層 DRAM 間以 TSV 互
連，公司提供 DRAM 方案，DRAM 本身仍以 3D 為主 
 與 SoC 的連接方式(含 2.5D)依客戶系統決定，公司 DRAM 端不
需太多改變，部分美國客戶已在測試 2.5D 
 客戶如何選擇商業模式，利潤率如何？  
 預估部分客戶明年進入量產， 量產規模放大後毛利率 可望更高，
發酵時點稍晚  
 客戶選擇與自身資源有關：缺乏後段管理外包商資源者傾向委由
公司處理，有能力者可能自行處理  
 明年量產的晶圓需求如何與晶圓廠洽談？2027 年量產專案使用現
有產線還是新廠？  
 直接關係為公司的客戶，實際為客戶/ 公司/南亞科的三方關係  
 專案啟動前須先與南亞科確認可接受客戶未來需求，各晶圓廠均
需如此洽談；南亞科每年固定詢問需求，近期因新廠開出而增加
確認次數 
 確切數量於下單時確認，大客戶通常提前半年至一年安排並持續
更新；南亞科擴產時會確認可供應量，無法供應的部分會告知一
至兩年後的供應安排  
 2027 年專案現有產線與新廠皆有機會，新廠 2H27 開出 
 合約負債的性質為何？  
 客戶 NRE 的收款方式與財報認列方式不同，財報依投入的時間
與人力成本認列，部分客戶預付大筆現金，記為合約負債 
 人力擴充計畫？  
 目前缺人而非缺錢，正積極招募 
 與客戶合作增加邏輯設計 /實體設計/custom cell 等需求，所需人
才與過去不同。 各階段人力需求不同，前期最多，專案後期釋出
的人力可轉投其他專案，部分部門前後期均需支援  
 客戶洽談先談規格與時程，待人力到位再正式啟動

### Page 6

台灣 補丁科

十月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 6

## Document 565: 凱基投顧_WFC Wells Fargo _ Co._施志鴻_20261014

### Page 1

十月 14, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  金融‧美國

Wells Fargo & Co.  
(WFC.N/WFC US)

3Q26 獲利略優於預期，但 4Q26F 營運展望疲弱 
重要訊息  
富國銀行 (WFC) 3Q26 每股盈餘 1.87 元，略優於市場及凱基預估 2%，主要
受惠於一次性所得稅迴轉帶動有效稅率降至 16.4% 。  
評論及 分析 
3Q26 投資收入減少及呆帳提存回升。 WFC 3Q26 稅後淨利為  59.5 億美
元，季減 7%、年增 6.5% ，主要非利息收入季減 9%，反映投資收益季減
52% 及投資銀行手收季減 3% ，抵銷放款及資產管理手收各季增 4% 及
8%；3Q26 核心獲利淨利息收入(NII)季增 1.6% 至 125.2 億美元，略低於市
場預期 1%。然而，3Q26 總營收年增 2%，高於營業費用年減 1%，正向營
運槓桿持續擴大，部分緩解呆帳提存年增 51% 至 10.3 億美元影響。 WFC 
3Q26 普通股權益比率(CET1) 季減 30 基點至 10% ， 位於管理區間 10-
10.5% 之區間下緣，主要 AOCI  之債券虧損影響約 38 基點。 
3Q26 淨利差持平， 呆帳沖銷率略為回升 。WFC 3Q26 淨利差 2.43% ，與上
季持平，相較先前公司 認為將下滑 4 基點， 主要放款維持 成長、固定利率資
產重訂價及浮動利率收益率提高，抵銷資金成本增加壓力 ，帶動存放利差持
平於 1.88% 。3Q26 放款年增率略減緩至 11%，主要來自企金及汽車貸款
各年增 19%及 31% ，總存款年增率下降至 10%，但計息存款增幅較高( 年
增 14%)。WFC 資產品質持續改善，3Q26 逾放比季減 0.07% 至 0.7% ，其
中企金逾放季減 0.1% 至 0.64% ，然 3Q26 整體呆帳沖銷率季增 4 基點至
0.38% ，主要商辦不動產帶動企金沖銷率增加 0.08% ，抵銷信用卡沖銷率
略降低 0.45% 至 3.73% 。 
維持 2026F 淨利息收入目標 。雖美國聯準會 9 月重啟升息，管理層維持
2026F 淨利息收入年增 5%至 500 億元， 預估 4Q26F 淨利差仍可 維持 3Q26
水準，並維持 2026 年營業費用 557 億元目標。此次公司尚未提供 2027 淨
利息收入展望， 若用 4Q26 年化單季淨利息收入約 為 520 億元，符合目前市
場預期， 但管理層認為存款 Beta 可能高於 2022 升息循環，故資金成本上揚
可能壓縮淨利差及淨利息收入。公司表示目前審慎評估 AI 基礎設施融資，
嚴格控管多層次風險( 資料中心建設、供電、晶片融資、承租方及保證責
任)，並設有承作上限。  
投資建議  
凱基維持富國銀行「持有」評等，目標價 90 美元，約 2026F -27F 平均每股
盈餘 12 倍(過去五年本益比區間為 5-17X)，主要認為 WFC 可能仍以犧牲 價
格策略以擴張孳息資產負債，加上淨利差回升疲弱，未來利率維持較高水準
仍將影響商辦不動產及消金資產品質，影響 2027F 獲利成長動能 。 
投資風險  
美國失業率增加、高利率持續影響銀行資產品質惡化； 全球金融市場轉弱。

圖 14 ：公司概況  圖 15：WFC 組織架構圖 
富國銀行（ Wells Fargo & Company ）創立於 1852 年，總部
位於美國加州舊金山。公司業務涵蓋消費金融、商業銀行、企
業與投資銀行、以及財富與資產管理，主要服務美國境內客
戶，提供從零售銀行到高資產客戶投資顧問的全方位金融服
務，部分國際市場提供選擇性業務。 2Q25 富國銀行資產規模
約為 1.98 兆美元，為全美第四大銀行，員工人數超過 22 萬
人。公司品牌以穩健為主，強調資本控管、風險管理及長期股
東回報，近年積極整頓監理問題並改善風控架構。

### Page 7

美國 Wells Fargo & Co.

十月 14, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 7

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary 再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
股東治理分數 衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性 
公司治理分數 衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性 
產品責任分數 衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量 
社區關係分數 衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾 
勞動力分數 衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效 
資源使用指標 衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案

## Document 568: 凱基投顧_電子硬體產業_余昀澄_20261012

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_電子硬體產業_余昀澄_20261012.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   1

產業報告  台灣

電子硬體產業 
PC 需求惡化，2027 年持續疲弱 
重要訊息  
3Q26 全球 PC 出貨量季減 14% 至 5,540 萬台，較我們預估低 8%。由於零
組件成本上升，促使品牌廠持續調漲 PC 售價，我們預 估 4Q26 全球 PC 出貨
量將再 季減 12%。我們保守看待全球 PC 需求，我們預 估 2026 年 PC 出貨
量衰退幅度擴大至年減 14%，2027 年則續減 12%。 
評論及分析    
3Q26 PC 出貨不如預期。 根據 Gartner 統計，3Q26 全球 PC 出貨量年減
24% 、季減 14% 至 5,540 萬台，較我們原估低 8% 。華碩 (2357 TT , 
NT$977, 增加持股) PC 出貨量季增 7%，符合公司展望，優於同業因電競
PC 比重較高，而此類客戶價格敏感度較低。然而，其餘品牌出貨量均明顯
季減。其中，Apple ( 美) 出貨量季減 23%，降幅最大，因消費者趕在 6 月
MacBook 價格調漲 15-20%前，於 1H26 提前購買。 HP ( 美) 因消費比重較
高，PC 出貨量季減 20% ；Dell (美)也因消費者提前購買而使 出貨量季減  
19% 。主要筆電 ODM 出貨亦受到拖累，其中仁寶 (2324 TT , NT$36.6, 未評
等) 3Q26 筆電出貨量季減 21%、緯創 (3231 TT , NT$189.5,  增加持股) 季減
18% 、廣達 (2382 TT , NT$322, 增加持股) 季減 17% 。整體而言，台灣前五
大筆電 ODM 出貨量合計季減 17%。區域別 來看，3Q26 亞太地區 (不含日
本) PC 出貨量僅季減 4%，優於其他地區，但 歐非中東季減 17% 、北美季
減 22%。1Q-3Q26 全球 PC 出貨量年減 8%，其中亞太地區 (不含日本) 僅
年減 1%，美國年減 5%，優於其他區域。1 -3Q26 各品牌中，Apple 出貨量
年增 12%，優於同業， 主因消費者於 1H26 提前購買。  
4Q26 PC 出貨量季減，下修 2026 年 PC 需求預估。 隨 DRAM 與 NAND 價格
3Q25 以來持續上漲，目前其成本占 PC BOM 比重已超過 40%，較 1Q25 的
15-20% 大幅提升。 1H26 Windows PC 零售價年增超過 30%，考量記憶體
及 CPU 等零組件成本持續攀升，我們預期 3Q-4Q26 PC 價 格 再季增 5-
10% ，進一步抑制 PC 需求。同時，我們觀察到 PC 品牌因消費需求疲弱，
2H26 削減 PC 零組件 (如 IC) 訂單。多數台灣筆電 ODM 預期 4Q26 筆電出
貨量持續季減，我們預估 4Q26 前五大筆電 ODM 筆電出貨量季減 12%。反
映消費需求低於預期，我們下修 4Q26 全球 PC 出貨量預估至季減 12%、年
減 33%，下修 2026 年全球 PC 出貨量至 2.32 億台，年減 14%。 
2027 年 PC 需求仍 疲軟。由於 1Q-4Q27 記憶體價格可能維持每季個位數漲
幅，PC 品牌為維持獲利能力，恐將持續調漲 PC 售價，或降低新機規格。此
外，2027 年 ODM 筆電新開案 數至少年減 10-20%，PC 品牌可能延長既有
機種銷售週期。綜上，我們 保守看待 2027 年 PC 需求，預期 1Q-2Q27 全球 
PC 季度出貨量大致與 4Q26 持平，隱含年減幅 28%。我們認為 PC 需求於
3Q-4Q27 才可望逐季復甦， 因低基期及新機上市使 旺季出貨量逐步回升。
我們下修 2027 年全球 PC 需求預估至 2.05 億台，年減 12%。  
投資建議  
在 PC 需求放緩的環境下，PC 品牌營收因 ASP 上揚，營收與獲利率受影響較
少。但我們對 PC 零組件供應鏈較保守看待，除營收面臨 PC 需求不如預期之衝
擊外，且因難以將上升成本完全轉嫁予品牌廠，獲利率恐進一步受到侵蝕。 
投資風險  
消費者需求疲弱、零組件價格攀升。 
焦點內容 
  1. 3Q26 全球 PC 出貨量季減 14% 至
5,540 萬台，較我們預估低 8% 。
3Q26 僅華碩 PC 出貨量季增 7%，優
於同業因電競 PC 比重較高。然而，蘋
果 (美)、HPQ ( 美)、Dell ( 美)則季減幅
度較大，連帶影響台灣 ODM 出貨量
3Q26 季減幅較大。 1Q-3Q26 全球 PC
出貨量年減 8%，僅 Apple 出貨量年增
12% ，優於同業，主因消費者 1H26
提前購買。 
2. 我們預期零組件價格上漲，將使品牌
廠 3Q-4Q26 持續調漲 PC 售價，此將
不利 PC 需求。我們同時 觀察到 PC 品
牌因消費需求疲弱， 2H26 削減 PC 零
組件 (如 IC) 訂單。我們下修 4Q26 全
球 PC 出貨量預估至季減 12% 、年減
33%，下修 2026 年全球 PC 出貨量至
2.32 億台，年減 14%。 
3. 由於 1Q-4Q27 記憶體價格可能維持每
季個位數漲幅，PC 品牌為維持獲利能
力，恐將持續調漲 PC 售價，或降低新
機規格。此外， 2027 年 ODM 筆電新
開案數至少年減 10-20%，PC 品牌可
能延長既有機種銷售週期。我們保守
看待 2027 年 PC 需求，下修 2027 年
全球 PC 需求預估至 2.05 億台，年減  
12%。

凱基投顧

### Page 2

台灣 電子硬體產業

十月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   2

圖 1：3Q26 全球 PC ( 含 Chromebook) 出貨量年減 24%、季減
14%，低於我們預估

20日均值43.2BP， 较昨日-0.2BP；5日均值41.9BP， 较昨日+0.0BP。 德债期限利差20日均值41.5BP， 较昨日+0.5BP；
5日均值39.2BP，较昨日-0.2BP。 
（06）股票市场：美股同比14.5%，较昨日-1.0个百分点；A股同比-10.8%，较昨日-2.3个百分点；欧股同比4.3%，
较昨日-1.3个百分点。

中国经济： 
（09）经济预期：经济意外指数20日均值-33.2，较昨日+2.2；5日均值-14.7，较昨日+7.0。 
（10）碳排成本：CEA碳排放权20日均值96.4，较昨日+0.1；5日均值97.8，较昨日+0.0。 
（11） 运输成本： 内贸集装箱运价指数1010.0， 较上周+0.90%，较去年同期-2.51%。 外贸集装箱进口运价指数119.3，
较上周+0.95%，较去年同期+16.95%。外贸集装箱出口运价指数160.9，较上周+0.54%，较去年同期+97.58%。 
（12）房地产：30城地产周度成交228.68万平，较上周+32.55%，较去年同期-19.35%。30城地产周度成交5周均值
222.03万平，较上周+2.88%，较去年同期-10.73%。

策略 
美国中期选举前的市场波动依旧。一方面，地缘不稳定性在俄乌战线出现降温可能，为中东战线提供空间；另一
方面，政治不稳定性在欧洲有扩散可能，为美国中期选举提供空间。结构性的转移并不意味着风险的结束，而是
压力的出口转变，市场的波动改善仍需要等待11月美国的结果落地。

风险 
美元流动性快速收紧
